      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {EnumerableSet} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/structs/EnumerableSet.sol\";\nimport {MoreVaultsDiamond} from \"../MoreVaultsDiamond.sol\";\nimport {MoreVaultsComposer} from \"../cross-chain/layerZero/MoreVaultsComposer.sol\";\nimport {IDiamondCut} from \"../interfaces/facets/IDiamondCut.sol\";\nimport {IMoreVaultsRegistry} from \"../interfaces/IMoreVaultsRegistry.sol\";\nimport {IVaultsFactory} from \"../interfaces/IVaultsFactory.sol\";\nimport {IVaultFacet} from \"../interfaces/facets/IVaultFacet.sol\";\nimport {\n    OAppUpgradeable,\n    Origin,\n    MessagingFee\n} from \"@layerzerolabs/oapp-evm-upgradeable/contracts/oapp/OAppUpgradeable.sol\";\nimport {\n    OAppOptionsType3Upgradeable\n} from \"@layerzerolabs/oapp-evm-upgradeable/contracts/oapp/libs/OAppOptionsType3Upgradeable.sol\";\nimport {CREATE3} from \"@solady/src/utils/CREATE3.sol\";\nimport {IConfigurationFacet} from \"../interfaces/facets/IConfigurationFacet.sol\";\nimport {IAccessControlFacet} from \"../interfaces/facets/IAccessControlFacet.sol\";\nimport {IBridgeAdapter} from \"../interfaces/IBridgeAdapter.sol\";\nimport {IMoreVaultsOftFactory} from \"../interfaces/IMoreVaultsOftFactory.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @title VaultsFactory\n * @notice Factory contract for deploying new vault instances\n */\ncontract VaultsFactory is IVaultsFactory, OAppUpgradeable, OAppOptionsType3Upgradeable {\n    using EnumerableSet for EnumerableSet.AddressSet;\n    using EnumerableSet for EnumerableSet.Bytes32Set;\n\n    /// @dev Thrown when non-vault tries to link or unlink facet.\n    error NotAuthorizedToLinkFacets(address);\n    /// @dev Thrown when non-vault tries to request cross-chain link\n    error NotAVault(address);\n    /// @dev Thrown when non-owner of vault tries to request cross-chain link\n    error NotAnOwnerOfVault(address);\n    /// @dev Thrown when max finalization time is not exceeded\n    error MaxFinalizationTimeNotExceeded();\n    /// @dev Thrown when hub cannot initiate cross-chain link\n    error HubCannotInitiateLink();\n    /// @dev Thrown when links are initiated from non-hub chain\n    error OnlyHub();\n    /// @dev Thrown when facet is restricted\n    error RestrictedFacet(address);\n    /// @dev Thrown when hub owner and spoke owner do not match\n    error OwnersMismatch(address hubOwner, address spokeOwner);\n    /// @dev Thrown when trying to register a second spoke from the same chain\n    error SpokeAlreadyExistsForChain(uint32 hubEid, address hubVault, uint32 spokeEid);\n    /// @dev Thrown on unknown message type\n    error UnknownMsgType();\n    /// @dev Thrown when hub and spoke addresses don't match (not in same mesh)\n    error VaultsNotInSameMesh();\n\n    /// @dev Registry contract address\n    IMoreVaultsRegistry public registry;\n\n    /// @dev DiamondCutFacet address\n    address public diamondCutFacet;\n\n    /// @dev AccessContorlFacet address\n    address public accessControlFacet;\n\n    /// @dev Mapping vault address => is deployed by this factory\n    mapping(address => bool) public isFactoryVault;\n\n    /// @dev Array of all deployed vaults\n    address[] public deployedVaults;\n\n    /// @dev Array of times of deployment of vaults\n    mapping(address => uint96) public deployedAt;\n\n    /// @dev Address of the wrapped native token\n    address public wrappedNative;\n\n    /// @dev Local LayerZero endpoint id (EID) for this chain\n    uint32 public localEid;\n\n    /// @dev Maximum finalization time of block for a chain\n    uint96 public maxFinalizationTime;\n\n    /// @dev Mapping spoke eid => spoke vault => packed hub (eid|address)\n    mapping(uint32 => mapping(address => bytes32)) private _spokeToHub;\n\n    /// @dev Mapping hub eid => hub vault => set of spokes (encoded as bytes32)\n    mapping(uint32 => mapping(address => EnumerableSet.Bytes32Set)) private _hubToSpokesSet;\n\n    /// @dev Address set of restricted facets\n    EnumerableSet.AddressSet private _restrictedFacets;\n\n    /// @dev Mapping facet address => vaults using this facet array\n    mapping(address => EnumerableSet.AddressSet) private _linkedVaults;\n\n    /// @dev Mapping vault address => vault composer address\n    mapping(address => address) public vaultComposer;\n\n    /// @dev Address of the LayerZero adapter\n    address public lzAdapter;\n\n    /// @dev Address of the MoreVaultsComposer implementation\n    address public composerImplementation;\n\n    /// @dev Address of the OFT factory for spoke vault shares\n    address public MoreVaultsOftFactory;\n    /// @dev Address of the OFT adapter factory for hub vault shares\n    address public MoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory;\n\n    // ===== Cross-chain messaging (LayerZero v2, EIDs) =====\n    uint16 private constant MSG_TYPE_REGISTER_SPOKE = 1;\n    uint16 private constant MSG_TYPE_SPOKE_ADDED = 2;\n    uint16 private constant MSG_TYPE_BOOTSTRAP = 3;\n\n    uint256 private _multiSendFee; // used to support multi-send in a single tx\n\n    constructor(address _endpoint) OAppUpgradeable(_endpoint) {}\n\n    function initialize(\n        address _owner,\n        address _registry,\n        address _diamondCutFacet,\n        address _accessControlFacet,\n        address _wrappedNative,\n        uint32 _localEid,\n        uint96 _maxFinalizationTime,\n        address _lzAdapter,\n        address _composerImplementation,\n        address _MoreVaultsOftFactory,\n        address _MoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory\n    ) external initializer {\n        if (\n            _owner == address(0) || _registry == address(0) || _diamondCutFacet == address(0)\n                || _accessControlFacet == address(0) || _wrappedNative == address(0) || _localEid == 0\n                || _MoreVaultsOftFactory == address(0) || _MoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory == address(0)\n        ) revert ZeroAddress();\n        _setDiamondCutFacet(_diamondCutFacet);\n        _setAccessControlFacet(_accessControlFacet);\n        _setMaxFinalizationTime(_maxFinalizationTime);\n        _setLzAdapter(_lzAdapter);\n        _setComposerImplementation(_composerImplementation);\n        _setMoreVaultsOftFactory(_MoreVaultsOftFactory);\n        _setMoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory(_MoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory);\n        wrappedNative = _wrappedNative;\n        registry = IMoreVaultsRegistry(_registry);\n        localEid = _localEid;\n        __OApp_init(_owner);\n        __Ownable_init(_owner);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Set the diamond cut facet address, that manages addition and removal of facets\n     * @param _diamondCutFacet The address of the diamond cut facet\n     */\n    function setDiamondCutFacet(address _diamondCutFacet) external onlyOwner {\n        _setDiamondCutFacet(_diamondCutFacet);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Set the access control facet address, that manages ownership and roles of the vault\n     * @param _accessControlFacet The address of the access control facet\n     */\n    function setAccessControlFacet(address _accessControlFacet) external onlyOwner {\n        _setAccessControlFacet(_accessControlFacet);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Set the maximum finalization time of block for a chain\n     * @param _maxFinalizationTime The maximum finalization time of block for a chain\n     */\n    function setMaxFinalizationTime(uint96 _maxFinalizationTime) external onlyOwner {\n        _setMaxFinalizationTime(_maxFinalizationTime);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice pauses all vaults using this facet\n     * @param _facet address of the facet\n     */\n    function pauseFacet(address _facet) external onlyOwner {\n        address[] memory vaults = _linkedVaults[_facet].values();\n        _setFacetRestricted(_facet, true);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < vaults.length;) {\n            try IVaultFacet(vaults[i]).pause() {}\n            catch (bytes memory) {\n                emit VaultFailedToPause(vaults[i]);\n            }\n            unchecked {\n                ++i;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice sets restricted flag for facet\n     * @param _facet address of facet\n     * @param _isRestricted bool flag\n     */\n    function setFacetRestricted(address _facet, bool _isRestricted) external onlyOwner {\n        _setFacetRestricted(_facet, _isRestricted);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @inheritdoc IVaultsFactory\n     */\n    function setLzAdapter(address _lzAdapter) external onlyOwner {\n        _setLzAdapter(_lzAdapter);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @inheritdoc IVaultsFactory\n     */\n    function setVaultComposer(address _vault, address _composer) external onlyOwner {\n        _setVaultComposer(_vault, _composer);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Set the OFT factory address used for spoke vaults\n     * @param _MoreVaultsOftFactory The address of the OFT factory\n     */\n    function setMoreVaultsOftFactory(address _MoreVaultsOftFactory) external onlyOwner {\n        _setMoreVaultsOftFactory(_MoreVaultsOftFactory);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Set the OFT adapter factory address used for hub vaults\n     * @param _MoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory The address of the OFT adapter factory\n     */\n    function setMoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory(address _MoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory) external onlyOwner {\n        _setMoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory(_MoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Set the MoreVaultsComposer implementation address\n     * @param _composerImplementation The address of the MoreVaultsComposer implementation\n     */\n    function setComposerImplementation(address _composerImplementation) external onlyOwner {\n        if (_composerImplementation == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        _setComposerImplementation(_composerImplementation);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice link the vault to the facet\n     * @param _facet address of the facet\n     */\n    function link(address _facet) external {\n        if (!isFactoryVault[msg.sender]) {\n            revert NotAuthorizedToLinkFacets(msg.sender);\n        }\n\n        _linkedVaults[_facet].add(msg.sender);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice unlink the vault from the facet\n     * @param _facet address of the facet\n     */\n    function unlink(address _facet) external {\n        if (!isFactoryVault[msg.sender]) {\n            revert NotAuthorizedToLinkFacets(msg.sender);\n        }\n        _linkedVaults[_facet].remove(msg.sender);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @inheritdoc IVaultsFactory\n     */\n    function deployVault(\n        IDiamondCut.FacetCut[] calldata facets,\n        bytes memory accessControlFacetInitData,\n        bool isHub,\n        bytes32 vaultIdentifier\n    ) external returns (address vault) {\n        // Salt derived from msg.sender + vaultIdentifier to ensure same address across chains\n        // and prevent front-running (each deployer has its own namespace)\n        bytes32 salt = keccak256(abi.encodePacked(msg.sender, vaultIdentifier));\n\n        // Deploy new MoreVaultsDiamond (vault) with CREATE3\n        vault = CREATE3.deployDeterministic(\n            abi.encodePacked(\n                type(MoreVaultsDiamond).creationCode,\n                abi.encode(\n                    diamondCutFacet,\n                    accessControlFacet,\n                    address(registry),\n                    wrappedNative,\n                    address(this),\n                    isHub,\n                    facets,\n                    accessControlFacetInitData\n                )\n            ),\n            salt\n        );\n        address shareOft = _deployOFT(vault, isHub, salt);\n        // Deploy minimal proxy for composer\n        if (isHub) {\n            address composer = _deployComposerProxy(vault, shareOft, salt);\n            _setVaultComposer(vault, address(composer));\n        }\n\n        isFactoryVault[vault] = true;\n        deployedVaults.push(vault);\n        deployedAt[vault] = uint96(block.timestamp);\n        _linkedVaults[diamondCutFacet].add(vault);\n        _linkedVaults[accessControlFacet].add(vault);\n        _checkRestrictedFacet(diamondCutFacet);\n        _checkRestrictedFacet(accessControlFacet);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < facets.length;) {\n            _checkRestrictedFacet(facets[i].facetAddress);\n            _linkedVaults[facets[i].facetAddress].add(vault);\n            unchecked {\n                ++i;\n            }\n        }\n        emit VaultDeployed(vault, address(registry), wrappedNative, facets);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Predict the address of a vault deployed with given salt (CREATE3).\n     */\n    function predictVaultAddress(bytes32 salt) external view returns (address) {\n        return CREATE3.predictDeterministicAddress(salt, address(this));\n    }\n\n    function _encodeSpokeKey(uint32 eid, address vault) internal pure returns (bytes32) {\n        return bytes32((uint256(eid) << 160) | uint160(vault));\n    }\n\n    function _decodeSpokeKey(bytes32 key) internal pure returns (uint32 eid, address vault) {\n        vault = address(uint160(uint256(key)));\n        eid = uint32(uint256(key) >> 160);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Spoke requests registration on Hub. EIDs must be used (not EVM chainId)\n    /// @param _hubEid LayerZero endpoint id of hub chain\n    /// @param _hubVault Address of hub vault on hub chain (local to hub)\n    /// @param _spokeVault Address of spoke vault on current chain\n    /// @param _options LZ options (type3). Can be empty; enforced options may apply\n    function requestRegisterSpoke(uint32 _hubEid, address _hubVault, address _spokeVault, bytes calldata _options)\n        external\n        payable\n    {\n        if (!isFactoryVault[_spokeVault]) revert NotAVault(_spokeVault);\n        if (IAccessControlFacet(_spokeVault).owner() != msg.sender) {\n            revert NotAnOwnerOfVault(msg.sender);\n        }\n        if (block.timestamp - deployedAt[_spokeVault] < maxFinalizationTime) {\n            revert MaxFinalizationTimeNotExceeded();\n        }\n\n        address spokeOwner = IAccessControlFacet(_spokeVault).owner();\n        bytes memory payload =\n            abi.encode(uint16(MSG_TYPE_REGISTER_SPOKE), abi.encode(_spokeVault, _hubVault, spokeOwner));\n\n        bytes memory options = combineOptions(_hubEid, MSG_TYPE_REGISTER_SPOKE, _options);\n        MessagingFee memory fee = _quote(_hubEid, payload, options, false);\n        if (msg.value < fee.nativeFee) {\n            revert NotEnoughNative(msg.value);\n        }\n        _lzSend(_hubEid, payload, options, fee, msg.sender);\n        emit CrossChainLinkRequested(_hubEid, msg.sender, _spokeVault, _hubVault);\n    }\n\n    function _lzReceive(\n        Origin calldata _origin,\n        bytes32, /* _guid */\n        bytes calldata _message,\n        address, /* _executor */\n        bytes calldata /* _extraData */\n    )\n        internal\n        override\n    {\n        // OAppReceiver already validated endpoint and peer\n        (uint16 msgType, bytes memory rest) = abi.decode(_message, (uint16, bytes));\n        if (msgType == MSG_TYPE_REGISTER_SPOKE) {\n            (address spokeVault, address hubVault, address spokeOwner) = abi.decode(rest, (address, address, address));\n\n            // Hub and spoke must have identical addresses (same mesh via CREATE3 deterministic deployment)\n            if (hubVault != spokeVault) revert VaultsNotInSameMesh();\n\n            // Ensure dst hub vault is actually deployed by this factory and is hub\n            if (!isFactoryVault[hubVault]) revert NotAVault(hubVault);\n            if (!IConfigurationFacet(hubVault).isHub()) {\n                revert HubCannotInitiateLink();\n            }\n\n            // owners must match: spoke owner (from trusted peer) and local hub owner\n            address hubOwner = IAccessControlFacet(hubVault).owner();\n            if (hubOwner != spokeOwner) {\n                revert OwnersMismatch(hubOwner, spokeOwner);\n            }\n\n            uint32 hubEid = localEid;\n            uint32 spokeEid = _origin.srcEid;\n\n            _updateConnections(hubEid, hubVault, spokeEid, spokeVault);\n            emit CrossChainLinked(spokeEid, spokeVault, hubVault);\n        } else if (msgType == MSG_TYPE_SPOKE_ADDED) {\n            // optional: update local caches on spokes when hub broadcasts new peers\n            (uint32 hubEid, address hubVault, uint32 newSpokeEid, address newSpokeVault) =\n                abi.decode(rest, (uint32, address, uint32, address));\n            // track that this spoke knows about new peer under its hub\n            _updateConnections(hubEid, hubVault, newSpokeEid, newSpokeVault);\n        } else if (msgType == MSG_TYPE_BOOTSTRAP) {\n            // Merge-only bootstrap: add missing spokes; do not remove existing ones\n            (uint32 hubEid, address hubVault, bytes32[] memory others) = abi.decode(rest, (uint32, address, bytes32[]));\n            for (uint256 i = 0; i < others.length; i++) {\n                (uint32 newSpokeEid, address newSpokeVault) = _decodeSpokeKey(others[i]);\n                _updateConnections(hubEid, hubVault, newSpokeEid, newSpokeVault);\n            }\n        } else {\n            revert UnknownMsgType();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Hub: send BOOTSTRAP snapshot to a single spoke (list of all known spokes)\n    function hubSendBootstrap(uint32 _dstEid, address _hubVault, bytes calldata _options) external payable {\n        if (!isFactoryVault[_hubVault]) revert NotAVault(_hubVault);\n        if (IAccessControlFacet(_hubVault).owner() != msg.sender) {\n            revert NotAnOwnerOfVault(msg.sender);\n        }\n        if (!IConfigurationFacet(_hubVault).isHub()) {\n            revert OnlyHub();\n        }\n\n        // build snapshot as packed keys\n        bytes32[] memory spokes = _hubToSpokesSet[localEid][_hubVault].values();\n        bytes memory payload = abi.encode(MSG_TYPE_BOOTSTRAP, abi.encode(localEid, _hubVault, spokes));\n        bytes memory options = combineOptions(_dstEid, MSG_TYPE_BOOTSTRAP, _options);\n        MessagingFee memory fee = _quote(_dstEid, payload, options, false);\n        if (msg.value < fee.nativeFee) {\n            revert NotEnoughNative(msg.value);\n        }\n        _lzSend(_dstEid, payload, options, fee, msg.sender);\n    }\n\n    /// @notice Hub: broadcast SPOKE_ADDED to the specified destination EIDs\n    function hubBroadcastSpokeAdded(\n        address _hubVault,\n        uint32 _newSpokeEid,\n        address _newSpokeVault,\n        uint32[] calldata _dstEids,\n        bytes calldata _options\n    ) external payable {\n        if (!isFactoryVault[_hubVault]) revert NotAVault(_hubVault);\n        if (IAccessControlFacet(_hubVault).owner() != msg.sender) {\n            revert NotAnOwnerOfVault(msg.sender);\n        }\n        if (!IConfigurationFacet(_hubVault).isHub()) {\n            revert OnlyHub();\n        }\n\n        bytes memory payload =\n            abi.encode(MSG_TYPE_SPOKE_ADDED, abi.encode(localEid, _hubVault, _newSpokeEid, _newSpokeVault));\n\n        _multiSendFee = msg.value;\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < _dstEids.length; i++) {\n            bytes memory options = combineOptions(_dstEids[i], MSG_TYPE_SPOKE_ADDED, _options);\n            MessagingFee memory fee = _quote(_dstEids[i], payload, options, false);\n            // On last iteration flush remaining budget so Endpoint refunds remainder to msg.sender\n            if (i + 1 == _dstEids.length) {\n                // Make _payNative send all remaining budget; it will be refunded by Endpoint\n                fee.nativeFee = _multiSendFee;\n            }\n            _lzSend(_dstEids[i], payload, options, fee, msg.sender);\n        }\n        _multiSendFee = 0;\n    }\n\n    // Support multiple _lzSend calls per tx by allocating from a shared native fee budget.\n    function _payNative(uint256 _nativeFee) internal override returns (uint256) {\n        // Check if it is multi send or not.\n        if (_multiSendFee == 0) {\n            if (msg.value < _nativeFee) revert NotEnoughNative(msg.value);\n            return _nativeFee;\n        }\n        if (_multiSendFee < _nativeFee) revert NotEnoughNative(_multiSendFee);\n        unchecked {\n            _multiSendFee -= _nativeFee;\n        }\n        return _nativeFee;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Get all deployed vaults\n     * @return Array of vault addresses\n     */\n    function getDeployedVaults() external view override returns (address[] memory) {\n        return deployedVaults;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Get number of deployed vaults\n     * @return Number of vaults\n     */\n    function getVaultsCount() external view override returns (uint256) {\n        return deployedVaults.length;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Returns vaults addresses using this facet\n     * @param _facet address of the facet\n     * @return vaults of the vaults that are linked to the facet\n     */\n    function getLinkedVaults(address _facet) external view returns (address[] memory vaults) {\n        vaults = _linkedVaults[_facet].values();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Returns facet addresses that are restricted\n     * @return facets addresses of the restricted facets\n     */\n    function getRestrictedFacets() external view returns (address[] memory facets) {\n        facets = _restrictedFacets.values();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Returns bool flag if vault linked to the facet\n     * @param _facet address of the facet\n     * @param _vault address of the vault\n     */\n    function isVaultLinked(address _facet, address _vault) external view returns (bool) {\n        return _linkedVaults[_facet].contains(_vault);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @inheritdoc IVaultsFactory\n     */\n    function hubToSpokes(uint32 __eid, address _hubVault)\n        external\n        view\n        returns (uint32[] memory eids, address[] memory vaults)\n    {\n        bytes32[] memory values = _hubToSpokesSet[__eid][_hubVault].values();\n        eids = new uint32[](values.length);\n        vaults = new address[](values.length);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {\n            (uint32 eid_, address vault_) = _decodeSpokeKey(values[i]);\n            eids[i] = eid_;\n            vaults[i] = vault_;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @inheritdoc IVaultsFactory\n     */\n    function isSpokeOfHub(uint32 _hubEid, address _hubVault, uint32 _spokeEid, address _spokeVault)\n        external\n        view\n        returns (bool)\n    {\n        return _hubToSpokesSet[_hubEid][_hubVault].contains(_encodeSpokeKey(_spokeEid, _spokeVault));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @inheritdoc IVaultsFactory\n     */\n    function isCrossChainVault(uint32 __eid, address _vault) external view returns (bool) {\n        return _hubToSpokesSet[__eid][_vault].length() > 0;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @inheritdoc IVaultsFactory\n     */\n    function spokeToHub(uint32 __eid, address _spokeVault) external view returns (uint32 eid, address vault) {\n        bytes32 value = _spokeToHub[__eid][_spokeVault];\n        if (value == bytes32(0)) return (0, address(0));\n        (eid, vault) = _decodeSpokeKey(value);\n    }\n\n    function _setDiamondCutFacet(address _diamondCutFacet) internal {\n        if (_diamondCutFacet == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        diamondCutFacet = _diamondCutFacet;\n        emit DiamondCutFacetUpdated(_diamondCutFacet);\n    }\n\n    function _setAccessControlFacet(address _accessControlFacet) internal {\n        if (_accessControlFacet == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        accessControlFacet = _accessControlFacet;\n        emit AccessControlFacetUpdated(_accessControlFacet);\n    }\n\n    function _setMaxFinalizationTime(uint96 _maxFinalizationTime) internal {\n        maxFinalizationTime = _maxFinalizationTime;\n        emit MaxFinalizationTimeUpdated(_maxFinalizationTime);\n    }\n\n    function _setFacetRestricted(address _facet, bool _isRestricted) private {\n        if (_isRestricted) _restrictedFacets.add(_facet);\n        else _restrictedFacets.remove(_facet);\n\n        emit SetFacetRestricted(_facet, _isRestricted);\n    }\n\n    function _setLzAdapter(address _lzAdapter) internal {\n        lzAdapter = _lzAdapter;\n        emit LzAdapterUpdated(_lzAdapter);\n    }\n\n    function _setVaultComposer(address _vault, address _composer) internal {\n        if (_vault == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        if (_composer == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        vaultComposer[_vault] = _composer;\n        emit VaultComposerUpdated(_vault, _composer);\n    }\n\n    function _setComposerImplementation(address _composerImplementation) internal {\n        composerImplementation = _composerImplementation;\n        emit ComposerImplementationUpdated(_composerImplementation);\n    }\n\n    function _setMoreVaultsOftFactory(address _MoreVaultsOftFactory) internal {\n        if (_MoreVaultsOftFactory == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        MoreVaultsOftFactory = _MoreVaultsOftFactory;\n        emit MoreVaultsOftFactoryUpdated(_MoreVaultsOftFactory);\n    }\n\n    function _setMoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory(address _MoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory) internal {\n        if (_MoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n        MoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory = _MoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory;\n        emit MoreVaultsOftAdapterFactoryUpdated(_MoreVaultsOftAdapterFactory);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Deploy vault share cross-chain token by vault type\n     * @param _vault The vault address (token for adapter on hub, source token for OFT on spoke)\n     * @param isHub Whether the vault is a hub vault\n     * @param _salt The salt for deterministic deployment\n     * @return The address of the deployed OFT or OFT adapter\n     */\n    function _deployOFT(address _vault, bool isHub, bytes32 _salt) internal returns (address) {\n        address targetFactory = isHub ? Developers should keep in mind that\n     * this function has an unbounded cost, and using it as part of a state-changing function may render the function\n     * uncallable if the set grows to a point where copying to memory consumes too much gas to fit in a block.\n     */\n    function values(StringSet storage set, uint256 start, uint256 end) internal view returns (string[] memory) {\n        unchecked {\n            end = Math.min(end, length(set));\n            start = Math.min(start, end);\n\n            uint256 len = end - start;\n            string[] memory result = new string[](len);\n            for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; ++i) {\n                result[i] = Arrays.unsafeAccess(set._values, start + i).value;\n            }\n            return result;\n        }\n    }\n\n    struct BytesSet {\n        // Storage of set values\n        bytes[] _values;\n        // Position is the index of the value in the `values` array plus 1.\n        // Position 0 is used to mean a value is not in the set.\n        mapping(bytes value => uint256) _positions;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Add a value to a set. O(1).\n     *\n     * Returns true if the value was removed from the set, that is if it was\n     * present.\n     */\n    function remove(BytesSet storage set, bytes memory value) internal returns (bool) {\n        // We cache the value's position to prevent multiple reads from the same storage slot\n        uint256 position = set._positions[value];\n\n        if (position != 0) {\n            // Equivalent to contains(set, value)\n            // To delete an element from the _values array in O(1), we swap the element to delete with the last one in\n            // the array, and then remove the last element (sometimes called as 'swap and pop').\n            // This modifies the order of the array, as noted in {at}.\n\n            uint256 valueIndex = position - 1;\n            uint256 lastIndex = set._values.length - 1;\n\n            if (valueIndex != lastIndex) {\n                bytes memory lastValue = set._values[lastIndex];\n\n                // Move the lastValue to the index where the value to delete is\n                set._values[valueIndex] = lastValue;\n                // Update the tracked position of the lastValue (that was just moved)\n                set._positions[lastValue] = position;\n            }\n\n            // Delete the slot where the moved value was stored\n            set._values.pop();\n\n            // Delete the tracked position for the deleted slot\n            delete set._positions[value];\n\n            return true;\n        } else {\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Removes all the values from a set. O(n).\n     *\n     * WARNING: Developers should keep in mind that this function has an unbounded cost and using it may render the\n     * function uncallable if the set grows to the point where clearing it consumes too much gas to fit in a block.\n     */\n    function clear(BytesSet storage set) internal {\n        uint256 len = length(set);\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; ++i) {\n            delete set._positions[set._values[i]];\n        }\n        Arrays.unsafeSetLength(set._values, 0);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns true if the value is in the set. O(1).\n     */\n    function contains(BytesSet storage set, bytes memory value) internal view returns (bool) {\n        return set._positions[value] != 0;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns the number of values on the set. O(1).\n     */\n    function length(BytesSet storage set) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        return set._values.length;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns the value stored at position `index` in the set. O(1).\n     *\n     * Note that there are no guarantees on the ordering of values inside the\n     * array, and it may change when more values are added or removed.\n     *\n     * Requirements:\n     *\n     * - `index` must be strictly less than {length}.\n     */\n    function at(BytesSet storage set, uint256 index) internal view returns (bytes memory) {\n        return set._values[index];\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Return the entire set in an array\n     *\n     * WARNING: This operation will copy the entire storage to memory, which can be quite expensive. This is designed\n     * to mostly be used by view accessors that are queried without any gas fees. Developers should keep in mind that\n     * this function has an unbounded cost, and using it as part of a state-changing function may render the function\n     * uncallable if the set grows to a point where copying to memory consumes too much gas to fit in a block.\n     */\n    function values(BytesSet storage set) internal view returns (bytes[] memory) {\n        return set._values;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Return a slice of the set in an array\n     *\n     * WARNING: This operation will copy the entire storage to memory, which can be quite expensive. This is designed\n     * to mostly be used by view accessors that are queried without any gas fees. Developers should keep in mind that\n     * this function has an unbounded cost, and using it as part of a state-changing function may render the function\n     * uncallable if the set grows to a point where copying to memory consumes too much gas to fit in a block.\n     */\n    function values(BytesSet storage set, uint256 start, uint256 end) internal view returns (bytes[] memory) {\n        unchecked {\n            end = Math.min(end, length(set));\n            start = Math.min(start, end);\n\n            uint256 len = end - start;\n            bytes[] memory result = new bytes[](len);\n            for (uint256 i = 0; i < len; ++i) {\n                result[i] = Arrays.unsafeAccess(set._values, start + i).value;\n            }\n            return result;\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },

\"./libraries/AccessControlLib.sol\";\nimport {IDiamondCut} from \"./interfaces/facets/IDiamondCut.sol\";\nimport {IAccessControlFacet} from \"./interfaces/facets/IAccessControlFacet.sol\";\n\ncontract MoreVaultsDiamond {\n    error NativeTokenNotAvailable();\n    error FunctionDoesNotExist();\n\n    constructor(\n        address _diamondCutFacet,\n        address _accessControlFacet,\n        address _registry,\n        address _wrappedNative,\n        address _factory,\n        bool _isHub,\n        IDiamondCut.FacetCut[] selector\n        address facet = ds.selectorToFacetAndPosition[msg.sig].facetAddress;\n        if (facet == address(0)) {\n            revert FunctionDoesNotExist();\n        }\n        // Execute external function from facet using delegatecall and return any value.\n        assembly {\n            // copy function selector and any arguments\n            calldatacopy(0, 0, calldatasize())\n            // execute function call using the facet\n            let result := delegatecall(gas(), facet, 0, calldatasize(), 0, 0)\n            // get any return value\n            returndatacopy(0, 0, returndatasize())\n            // return any return value or error back to the caller\n            switch result\n            case 0 { revert(0, returndatasize()) }\n            default { return(0, returndatasize()) }\n        }\n    }\n\n    receive() external payable {\n        MoreVaultsLib.MoreVaultsStorage storage ds = MoreVaultsLib.moreVaultsStorage();\n        if (!ds.isAssetAvailable[ds.wrappedNative]) {\n            revert NativeTokenNotAvailable();\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: BUSL-1.1\npragma solidity 0.8.28;\n\nimport {SafeERC20, IERC20} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol\";\nimport {ERC4626, IERC4626} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/extensions/ERC4626.sol\";\n\nimport {ReentrancyGuard} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol\";\n\nimport {IOFT, SendParam, MessagingFee, OFTReceipt} from \"@layerzerolabs/oft-evm/contracts/interfaces/IOFT.sol\";\nimport {IOAppCore} from \"@layerzerolabs/oapp-evm/contracts/oapp/interfaces/IOAppCore.sol\";\nimport {ILayerZeroEndpointV2} from \"@layerzerolabs/lz-evm-protocol-v2/contracts/interfaces/ILayerZeroEndpointV2.sol\";\nimport {OFTComposeMsgCodec} from \"@layerzerolabs/oft-evm/contracts/libs/OFTComposeMsgCodec.sol\";\nimport {Initializable} from \"@openzeppelin/contracts/proxy/utils/Initializable.sol\";\nimport {IMoreVaultsComposer} from \"../../interfaces/LayerZero/IMoreVaultsComposer.sol\";\nimport {IBridgeFacet} from \"../../interfaces/facets/IBridgeFacet.sol\";\nimport {MoreVaultsLib} from \"../../libraries/MoreVaultsLib.sol\";\nimport {LzAdapter} from \"./LzAdapter.sol\";\nimport {IConfigurationFacet} from \"../../interfaces/facets/IConfigurationFacet.sol\";\nimport {IVaultFacet} from \"../../interfaces/facets/IVaultFacet.sol\";\nimport {IAccessControlFacet} from \"../../interfaces/facets/IAccessControlFacet.sol\";\nimport {IVaultsFactory} from \"../../interfaces/IVaultsFactory.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @title MoreVaultsComposer - MoreVaults Composer (deposit-only)\n * @notice Cross-chain composer that supports only cross chain deposits of assets from spoke chains to the hub vault.\n * @dev Refunds are enabled to EOA addresses only on the source chain.\n */\ncontract MoreVaultsComposer is IMoreVaultsComposer, ReentrancyGuard, Initializable {\n    using OFTComposeMsgCodec for bytes;\n    using OFTComposeMsgCodec for bytes32;\n    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;\n\n    IVaultFacet public VAULT;\n    IVaultsFactory public VAULT_FACTORY;\n    address public SHARE_OFT;\n    address public SHARE_ERC20;\n\n    address public ENDPOINT;\n    uint32 public VAULT_EID;\n\n    /// @dev Mapping from deposit ID to pending deposit info\n    mapping(bytes32 => PendingDeposit) internal _pendingDeposits;\n\n    /// @dev Total native pending amount (native currency locked in the composer to facilitate the async flow)\n    uint256 public totalNativePending;\n\n    // Async deposit lifecycle is tracked via callbacks and the Deposited event in the interface\n    constructor() {\n        _disableInitializers();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Initializes the proxy with vault and OFT token addresses\n     * @param _vault The address of the MoreVaults vault contract\n     * @param _shareOFT The address of the share OFT contract (must be an adapter)\n     * @param _vaultFactory The address of the vault factory contract\n     *\n     * Requirements:\n     * - Share token must be the vault itself\n     * - Share OFT must be an adapter (approvalRequired() returns true)\n     */\n    function initialize(address _vault, address _shareOFT, address _vaultFactory) external initializer {\n        VAULT = IVaultFacet(_vault);\n        SHARE_OFT = _shareOFT;\n        SHARE_ERC20 = IOFT(SHARE_OFT).token();\n        VAULT_FACTORY = IVaultsFactory(_vaultFactory);\n        ENDPOINT = address(IOAppCore(SHARE_OFT).endpoint());\n        VAULT_EID = ILayerZeroEndpointV2(ENDPOINT).eid();\n\n        // Validate initialization\n        if (SHARE_ERC20 != address(VAULT)) {\n            revert ShareTokenNotVault(SHARE_ERC20, address(VAULT));\n        }\n\n        /// @dev ShareOFT must be an OFT adapter. We can infer this by checking 'approvalRequired()'.\n        /// @dev burn() on tokens when a user sends changes totalSupply() which the asset:share ratio depends on.\n        if (!IOFT(SHARE_OFT).approvalRequired()) {\n            revert ShareOFTNotAdapter(SHARE_OFT);\n        }\n\n        /// @dev Approve the share adapter with the share tokens held by this contract\n        IERC20(SHARE_ERC20).forceApprove(_shareOFT, type(uint256).max);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Quotes the send operation for the given OFT and SendParam\n     * @dev Revert on slippage will be thrown by the OFT and not _assertSlippage\n     * @param _from The \"sender address\" used for the quote\n     * @param _targetOFT The OFT contract address to quote\n     * @param _vaultInAmount The amount of tokens to send to the vault\n     * @param _sendParam The parameters for the send operation\n     * @return MessagingFee The estimated fee for the send operation\n     * @dev This function can be overridden to implement custom quoting logic\n     */\n    function quoteSend(address _from, address _targetOFT, uint256 _vaultInAmount, SendParam memory _sendParam)\n        external\n        view\n        virtual\n        returns (MessagingFee memory)\n    {\n        /// @dev Only deposit flow is supported; quoting is only valid for SHARE_OFT (hub to destination hop)\n        if (_targetOFT != SHARE_OFT) revert NotImplemented();\n\n        uint256 maxDeposit = VAULT.maxDeposit(_from);\n        if (_vaultInAmount > maxDeposit) {\n            revert ERC4626.ERC4626ExceededMaxDeposit(_from, _vaultInAmount, maxDeposit);\n        }\n\n        _sendParam.amountLD = VAULT.previewDeposit(_vaultInAmount);\n        return IOFT(_targetOFT).quoteSend(_sendParam, false);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Handles LayerZero compose operations for vault transactions with automatic refund functionality\n     * @dev This composer is designed to handle refunds to an EOA address and not a contract\n     * @dev Any revert in handleCompose() causes a refund back to the src EXCEPT for InsufficientMsgValue\n     * @param _composeSender The OFT contract address used for refunds, must OFT that registered on the LZ_ADAPTER and corresponds to an asset that is depositable\n     * @param _guid LayerZero's unique tx id (created on the source tx)\n     * @param _message Decomposable bytes object into [composeHeader][composeMessage]\n     */\n    function lzCompose(\n        address _composeSender, // The OFT used on refund, also the vaultIn token.\n        bytes32 _guid,\n        bytes calldata _message, // expected to contain a composeMessage = abi.encode(SendParam hopSendParam,uint256 minMsgValue)\n        address, /*_executor*/\n        bytes calldata /*_extraData*/\n    )\n        external\n        payable\n        virtual\n        override\n    {\n        if (msg.sender != ENDPOINT) revert OnlyEndpoint(msg.sender);\n        if (\n            !LzAdapter(payable(VAULT_FACTORY.lzAdapter())).isTrustedOFT(_composeSender)\n                || !IConfigurationFacet(address(VAULT)).isAssetDepositable(IOFT(_composeSender).token())\n        ) {\n            revert InvalidComposeCaller(_composeSender);\n        }\n\n        bytes32 composeFrom = _message.composeFrom();\n        uint256 amount = _message.amountLD();\n        bytes memory composeMsg = _message.composeMsg();\n        uint32 srcEid = OFTComposeMsgCodec.srcEid(_message);\n\n        /// @dev try...catch to handle the compose operation. if it fails we refund the user\n        try this.handleCompose{value: msg.value}(_composeSender, composeFrom, composeMsg, amount, srcEid) {\n            emit Sent(_guid);\n        } catch (bytes memory _err) {\n            /// @dev A revert where the msg.value passed is lower than the min expected msg.value is handled separately\n            /// This is because it is possible to re-trigger from the endpoint the compose operation with the right msg.value\n            if (bytes4(_err) == InsufficientMsgValue.selector) {\n                assembly {\n                    revert(add(32, _err), mload(_err))\n                }\n            }\n\n            _refund(_composeSender, _message, amount, tx.origin);\n            emit Refunded(_guid);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Handles the compose operation for OFT (Omnichain Fungible Token) transactions\n     * @dev This function can only be called by the contract itself (self-call restriction)\n     *      Decodes the compose message to extract SendParam and minimum message value\n     *      Routes to either deposit or redeem flow based on the input OFT token type\n     * @param _oftIn The OFT token whose funds have been received in the lzReceive associated with this lzTx\n     * @param _composeFrom The bytes32 identifier of the compose sender\n     * @param _composeMsg The encoded message containing SendParam and minMsgValue\n     * @param _amount The amount of tokens received in the lzReceive associated with this lzTx\n     */\n    function handleCompose(\n        address _oftIn,\n        bytes32 _composeFrom,\n        bytes memory _composeMsg,\n        uint256 _amount,\n        uint32 _srcEid\n    ) external payable {\n        /// @dev Can only be called by self\n        if (msg.sender != address(this)) revert OnlySelf(msg.sender);\n\n        /// @dev SendParam defines how the composer will handle the user's funds\n        /// @dev The minMsgValue is the minimum amount of msg.value that must be sent, failing to do so will revert and the transaction will be retained in the endpoint for future retries\n        (SendParam memory sendParam, uint256 minMsgValue) = abi.decode(_composeMsg, (SendParam, uint256));\n        if (msg.value < minMsgValue) {\n            revert InsufficientMsgValue(minMsgValue, msg.value);\n        }\n        if (IVaultFacet(address(VAULT)).paused()) {\n            revert VaultIsPaused();\n        }\n        // Check if this is a cross-chain vault and oracle accounting is disabled\n        // If oracle accounting is enabled, use sync path even for cross-chain vaults\n        bool isCrossChainVault = VAULT_FACTORY.isCrossChainVault(uint32(VAULT_EID), address(VAULT));\n        bool useAsyncFlow = isCrossChainVault && !IBridgeFacet(address(VAULT)).oraclesCrossChainAccounting();\n\n        if (useAsyncFlow) {\n            _initDeposit(_composeFrom, IOFT(_oftIn).token(), _oftIn, _amount, sendParam, tx.origin, _srcEid);\n        } else {\n            _depositAndSend(_composeFrom, IOFT(_oftIn).token(), _amount, sendParam, tx.origin);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Sends deposit shares after successful request execution\n     * @param _guid The unique identifier of the pending deposit\n     * @dev This function is called after the request action has been executed successfully\n     * @dev Retrieves the execution result (shares) and sends them to the destination\n     */\n    function sendDepositShares(bytes32 _guid) external virtual nonReentrant {\n        if (msg.sender != address(VAULT) && msg.sender != address(VAULT_FACTORY.lzAdapter())) {\n            revert OnlyVaultOrLzAdapter(msg.sender);\n        }\n\n        PendingDeposit memory deposit = _pendingDeposits[_guid];\n        if (deposit.assetAmount == 0) revert DepositNotFound(_guid);\n\n        // Request action already executed in executeRequest\n        // Slippage check was already performed in _executeRequest\n        // Get execution result (number of shares)\n        uint256 shares = IBridgeFacet(address(VAULT)).getFinalizationResult(_guid);\n        deposit.sendParam.amountLD = shares;\n        deposit.sendParam.minAmountLD = 0;\n\n        delete _pendingDeposits[_guid];\n\n        uint256 amountSentLD = _send(SHARE_OFT, deposit.sendParam, deposit.refundAddress, deposit.msgValue);\n        totalNativePending -= deposit.msgValue;\n        emit Deposited(\n            deposit.depositor, deposit.sendParam.to, deposit.sendParam.dstEid, deposit.assetAmount, amountSentLD\n        );\n    }\n\n    function refundDeposit(bytes32 _guid) external payable virtual nonReentrant {\n        if (msg.sender != address(VAULT) && msg.sender != address(VAULT_FACTORY.lzAdapter())) {\n            revert OnlyVaultOrLzAdapter(msg.sender);\n        }\n        PendingDeposit memory deposit = _pendingDeposits[_guid];\n        if (deposit.assetAmount == 0) revert DepositNotFound(_guid);\n\n        delete _pendingDeposits[_guid];\n\n        // Tokens are already transferred and locked in vault by _lockFundsForRequest\n        // BridgeFacet.refundStuckDepositInComposer will unlock them via _unlockRequestFunds\n        // and transfer them back to composer via _transferTokensBackToComposer\n        // Tokens should already be in composer at this point\n\n        // cross-chain refund back to origin\n        SendParam memory refundSendParam;\n        refundSendParam.dstEid = deposit.srcEid;\n        refundSendParam.to = deposit.depositor;\n        refundSendParam.amountLD = deposit.assetAmount;\n\n        // Combine stored msgValue with additional msg.value to handle fee volatility\n        uint256 totalMsgValue = deposit.msgValue + msg.value;\n\n        IERC20(deposit.tokenAddress).forceApprove(deposit.oftAddress, deposit.assetAmount);\n        IOFT(deposit.oftAddress).send{value: totalMsgValue}(\n            refundSendParam, MessagingFee(totalMsgValue, 0), deposit.refundAddress\n        );\n        IERC20(deposit.tokenAddress).forceApprove(deposit.oftAddress, 0);\n        totalNativePending -= deposit.msgValue;\n        emit Refunded(_guid);\n    }\n\n    function depositAndSend(\n        address _tokenAddress,\n        uint256 _assetAmount,\n        SendParam memory _sendParam,\n        address _refundAddress\n    ) external payable virtual nonReentrant {\n        IERC20(_tokenAddress).safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), _assetAmount);\n        _depositAndSend(\n            OFTComposeMsgCodec.addressToBytes32(msg.sender), _tokenAddress, _assetAmount, _sendParam, _refundAddress\n        );\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @param _amountLD The amount of tokens to send\n     * @param _minAmountLD The minimum amount of tokens that must be sent to avoid slippage\n     * @notice This function checks if the amount sent is less than the minimum amount\n     *         If it is, it reverts with SlippageExceeded error\n     * @notice This function can be overridden to implement custom slippage logic\n     */\n    function _assertSlippage(uint256 _amountLD, uint256 _minAmountLD) internal view virtual {\n        if (_amountLD < _minAmountLD) {\n            revert SlippageExceeded(_amountLD, _minAmountLD);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Internal function to sync deposit assets into the vault and send shares to the destination\n     * @param _depositor The depositor (bytes32 format to account for non-evm addresses)\n     * @param _tokenAddress The address of the token to deposit\n     * @param _assetAmount The number of assets to deposit\n     * @param _sendParam Parameter that defines how to send the shares\n     */\n    function _depositAndSend(\n        bytes32 _depositor,\n        address _tokenAddress,\n        uint256 _assetAmount,\n        SendParam memory _sendParam,\n        address _refundAddress\n    ) internal virtual {\n        uint256 shareAmount;\n        IERC20(_tokenAddress).forceApprove(address(VAULT), _assetAmount);\n        if (_tokenAddress == IERC4626(VAULT).asset()) {\n            shareAmount = VAULT.deposit(_assetAmount, address(this));\n            _assertSlippage(shareAmount, _sendParam.minAmountLD);\n        } else {\n            address[] memory tokens = new address[](1);\n            tokens[0] = _tokenAddress;\n            uint256[] memory assets = new uint256[](1);\n            assets[0] = _assetAmount;\n            shareAmount = VAULT.deposit(tokens, assets, address(this), _sendParam.minAmountLD);\n        }\n        IERC20(_tokenAddress).forceApprove(address(VAULT), 0);\n\n        _sendParam.amountLD = shareAmount;\n        _sendParam.minAmountLD = 0;\n\n        uint256 amountSentLD = _send(SHARE_OFT, _sendParam, _refundAddress, msg.value);\n        emit Deposited(_depositor, _sendParam.to, _sendParam.dstEid, _assetAmount, amountSentLD);\n    }\n\n    function initDeposit(\n        bytes32 _depositor,\n        address _tokenAddress,\n        address _oftAddress,\n        uint256 _assetAmount,\n        SendParam memory _sendParam,\n        address _refundAddress,\n        uint32 _srcEid\n    ) external payable virtual nonReentrant {\n        IERC20(_tokenAddress).safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), _assetAmount);\n        _initDeposit(_depositor, _tokenAddress, _oftAddress, _assetAmount, _sendParam, _refundAddress, _srcEid);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Returns the pending deposit info for the given guid\n     * @param guid The guid of the pending deposit\n     * @return The pending deposit info\n     */\n    function pendingDeposits(bytes32 guid) external view returns (PendingDeposit memory) {\n        return _pendingDeposits[guid];\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Rescue accumulated dust tokens that remain locked due to LayerZero's decimal normalization\n     * @dev LayerZero normalizes token amounts to sharedDecimals (6 decimals), which truncates\n     *      the least significant digits for tokens with higher precision (e.g., 18 decimals).\n     *      This dust accumulates in the adapter contract and cannot be recovered through normal operations.\n     * @param _token The address of the token to rescue (use address(0) for native currency/ETH)\n     * @param _to The address to send the rescued tokens to\n     * @param _amount The amount of tokens to rescue (use type(uint256).max to rescue all available balance)\n     */\n    function rescue(address _token, address payable _to, uint256 _amount) external {\n        if (IAccessControlFacet(address(VAULT)).owner() != msg.sender) revert Unauthorized();\n        if (_to == address(0)) revert ZeroAddress();\n\n        if (_token == address(0)) {\n            // Rescue native currency (ETH)\n            uint256 availableBalance = address(this).balance - totalNativePending;\n            uint256 amountToRescue = _amount == type(uint256).max ? availableBalance : _amount;\n            if (amountToRescue > availableBalance) revert InsufficientBalance();\n\n            (bool success,) = _to.call{value: amountToRescue}(\"\");\n            if (!success) revert NativeTransferFailed();\n            emit Rescued(address(0), _to, amountToRescue);\n        } else {\n            // Rescue ERC20 token\n            uint256 availableBalance = IERC20(_token).balanceOf(address(this));\n            uint256 amountToRescue = _amount == type(uint256).max ? availableBalance : _amount;\n            if (amountToRescue > availableBalance) revert InsufficientBalance();\n\n            IERC20(_token).safeTransfer(_to, amountToRescue);\n            emit Rescued(_token, _to, amountToRescue);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Internal function that initiates an async deposit operation\n     * @param _depositor The depositor (bytes32 format to account for non-evm addresses)\n     * @param _tokenAddress The address of the token to deposit\n     * @param _oftAddress The address of the OFT contract to use for sending\n     * @param _assetAmount The number of assets to deposit\n     * @param _sendParam Parameter that defines how to send the shares\n     * @param _refundAddress Address to receive excess payment of the LZ fees\n     * @param _srcEid The source endpoint ID\n     * @notice This function first deposits the assets to mint shares, validates the shares meet minimum slippage requirements,\n     *         then sends the minted shares cross-chain using the OFT (Omnichain Fungible Token) protocol\n     * @notice The _sendParam.amountLD is updated to the actual share amount minted, and minAmountLD is reset to 0 for the send operation\n     */\n    function _initDeposit(\n        bytes32 _depositor,\n        address _tokenAddress,\n        address _oftAddress,\n        uint256 _assetAmount,\n        SendParam memory _sendParam,\n        address _refundAddress,\n        uint32 _srcEid\n    ) internal virtual {\n        uint256 readFee = IBridgeFacet(address(VAULT)).quoteAccountingFee(\"\");\n        if (msg.value < readFee) {\n            revert InsufficientMsgValue(readFee, msg.value);\n        }\n        if (IOFT(_oftAddress).token() != _tokenAddress) {\n            revert NotATokenOfOFT();\n        }\n\n        // Escrow pulls tokens from initiator (this composer) during BridgeFacet.initVaultActionRequest -> escrow.lockTokens().\n        // Therefore, approval must be granted to escrow (spender), not the VAULT.\n        address escrow = IConfigurationFacet(address(VAULT)).getEscrow();\n        if (escrow == address(0)) revert MoreVaultsLib.EscrowNotSet();\n        IERC20(_tokenAddress).forceApprove(escrow, _assetAmount);\n\n        MoreVaultsLib.ActionType actionType;\n        bytes memory actionCallData;\n        if (_tokenAddress == IERC4626(VAULT).asset()) {\n            actionType = MoreVaultsLib.ActionType.DEPOSIT;\n            actionCallData = abi.encode(uint256(_assetAmount), address(this));\n        } else {\n            actionType = MoreVaultsLib.ActionType.MULTI_ASSETS_DEPOSIT;\n            address[] memory tokens = new address[](1);\n            tokens[0] = _tokenAddress;\n            uint256[] memory assets = new uint256[](1);\n            assets[0] = _assetAmount;\n            uint256 minAmountOut = _sendParam.minAmountLD;\n            actionCallData = abi.encode(tokens, assets, address(this), minAmountOut, 0);\n        }\n        // Pass amountLimit for slippage check in _executeRequest\n        // Tokens will be transferred and locked inside initVaultActionRequest via escrow.lockTokens()\n        bytes32 guid = IBridgeFacet(address(VAULT)).initVaultActionRequest{value: readFee}(\n            actionType, actionCallData, _sendParam.minAmountLD, \"\"\n        );\n        \n        // Clear approval to minimize token approvals surface area.\n        IERC20(_tokenAddress).forceApprove(escrow, 0);\n        _pendingDeposits[guid] = PendingDeposit(\n            _depositor,\n            _tokenAddress,\n            _oftAddress,\n            _assetAmount,\n            _refundAddress,\n            msg.value - readFee,\n            _srcEid,\n            _sendParam\n        );\n\n        totalNativePending += msg.value - readFee;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Internal function that handles token transfer to the recipient\n     * @dev If the destination eid is the same as the current eid, it transfers the tokens directly to the recipient\n     * @dev If the destination eid is different, it sends a LayerZero cross-chain transaction\n     * @param _oft The OFT contract address to use for sending\n     * @param _sendParam The parameters for the send operation\n     * @param _refundAddress Address to receive excess payment of the LZ fees\n     * @return amountSentLD The amount actually sent (after LayerZero normalization for cross-chain, equal to amountLD for local)\n     */\n    function _send(address _oft, SendParam memory _sendParam, address _refundAddress, uint256 _msgValue)\n        internal\n        returns (uint256 amountSentLD)\n    {\n        if (_sendParam.dstEid == VAULT_EID) {\n            if (msg.value > 0) revert NoMsgValueExpected();\n            IERC20(SHARE_ERC20).safeTransfer(_sendParam.to.bytes32ToAddress(), _sendParam.amountLD);\n            return _sendParam.amountLD;\n        } else {\n            // crosschain send - LayerZero normalizes the amount, so we get the actual sent amount from the receipt\n            (, OFTReceipt memory oftReceipt) =\n                IOFT(_oft).send{value: _msgValue}(_sendParam, MessagingFee(_msgValue, 0), _refundAddress);\n            return oftReceipt.amountSentLD;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Internal function to refund input tokens to sender on source during a failed transaction\n     * @param _oft The OFT contract address used for refunding\n     * @param _message The original message that was sent\n     * @param _amount The amount of tokens to refund\n     * @param _refundAddress Address to receive the refund\n     */\n    function _refund(address _oft, bytes calldata _message, uint256 _amount, address _refundAddress) internal virtual {\n        /// @dev Extracted from the _message header. Will always be part of the _message since it is created by lzReceive\n        SendParam memory refundSendParam;\n        refundSendParam.dstEid = OFTComposeMsgCodec.srcEid(_message);\n        refundSendParam.to = OFTComposeMsgCodec.composeFrom(_message);\n        refundSendParam.amountLD = _amount;\n\n        IERC20(IOFT(_oft).token()).forceApprove(_oft, _amount);\n        IOFT(_oft).send{value: msg.value}(refundSendParam, MessagingFee(msg.value, 0), _refundAddress);\n        IERC20(IOFT(_oft).token()).forceApprove(_oft, 0);\n    }\n\n    receive() external payable {}\n}\n"

    "lib/devtools/packages/oapp-evm-upgradeable/contracts/oapp/libs/OAppOptionsType3Upgradeable.sol": {
      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\n\npragma solidity ^0.8.20;\n\nimport { OwnableUpgradeable } from \"@openzeppelin/contracts-upgradeable/access/OwnableUpgradeable.sol\";\nimport { IOAppOptionsType3, EnforcedOptionParam } from \"@layerzerolabs/oapp-evm/contracts/oapp/interfaces/IOAppOptionsType3.sol\";\n\n/**\n * @title OAppOptionsType3\n * @dev Abstract contract implementing the IOAppOptionsType3 interface with type 3 options.\n */\nabstract contract OAppOptionsType3Upgradeable is IOAppOptionsType3, OwnableUpgradeable {\n    struct OAppOptionsType3Storage {\n        // @dev The \"msgType\" should be defined in the child contract.\n        mapping(uint32 => mapping(uint16 => bytes)) enforcedOptions;\n    }\n\n    // keccak256(abi.encode(uint256(keccak256(\"layerzerov2.storage.oappoptionstype3\")) - 1)) & ~bytes32(uint256(0xff))\n    bytes32 private constant OAPP_OPTIONS_TYPE_3_STORAGE_LOCATION =\n        0x8d2bda5d9f6ffb5796910376005392955773acee5548d0fcdb10e7c264ea0000;\n\n    uint16 internal constant OPTION_TYPE_3 = 3;\n\n    function _getOAppOptionsType3Storage() internal pure returns (OAppOptionsType3Storage storage $) {\n        assembly {\n            $.slot := OAPP_OPTIONS_TYPE_3_STORAGE_LOCATION\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Ownable is not initialized here on purpose. It should be initialized in the child contract to\n     * accommodate the different version of Ownable.\n     */\n    function __OAppOptionsType3_init() internal onlyInitializing {}\n\n    function __OAppOptionsType3_init_unchained() internal onlyInitializing {}\n\n    function enforcedOptions(uint32 _eid, uint16 _msgType) public view returns (bytes memory) {\n        OAppOptionsType3Storage storage $ = _getOAppOptionsType3Storage();\n        return $.enforcedOptions[_eid][_msgType];\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Sets the enforced options for specific endpoint and message type combinations.\n     * @param _enforcedOptions An array of EnforcedOptionParam structures specifying enforced options.\n     *\n     * @dev Only the owner/admin of the OApp can call this function.\n     * @dev Provides a way for the OApp to enforce things like paying for PreCrime, AND/OR minimum dst lzReceive gas amounts etc.\n     * @dev These enforced options can vary as the potential options/execution on the remote may differ as per the msgType.\n     * eg. Amount of lzReceive() gas necessary to deliver a lzCompose() message adds overhead you dont want to pay\n     * if you are only making a standard LayerZero message ie. lzReceive() WITHOUT sendCompose().\n     */\n    function setEnforcedOptions(EnforcedOptionParam[] calldata _enforcedOptions) public virtual onlyOwner {\n        OAppOptionsType3Storage storage $ = _getOAppOptionsType3Storage();\n        for (uint256 i = 0; i < _enforcedOptions.length; i++) {\n            // @dev Enforced options are only available for optionType 3, as type 1 and 2 dont support combining.\n            _assertOptionsType3(_enforcedOptions[i].options);\n            $.enforcedOptions[_enforcedOptions[i].eid][_enforcedOptions[i].msgType] = _enforcedOptions[i].options;\n        }\n\n        emit EnforcedOptionSet(_enforcedOptions);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Combines options for a given endpoint and message type.\n     * @param _eid The endpoint ID.\n     * @param _msgType The OAPP message type.\n     * @param _extraOptions Additional options passed by the caller.\n     * @return options The combination of caller specified options AND enforced options.\n     *\n     * @dev If there is an enforced lzReceive option:\n     * - {gasLimit: 200k, msg.value: 1 ether} AND a caller supplies a lzReceive option: {gasLimit: 100k, msg.value: 0.5 ether}\n     * - The resulting options will be {gasLimit: 300k, msg.value: 1.5 ether} when the message is executed on the remote lzReceive() function.\n     * @dev This presence of duplicated options is handled off-chain in the verifier/executor.\n     */\n    function combineOptions(\n        uint32 _eid,\n        uint16 _msgType,\n        bytes calldata _extraOptions\n    ) public view virtual returns (bytes memory) {\n        OAppOptionsType3Storage storage $ = _getOAppOptionsType3Storage();\n        bytes memory enforced = $.enforcedOptions[_eid][_msgType];\n\n        // No enforced options, pass whatever the caller supplied, even if it's empty or legacy type 1/2 options.\n        if (enforced.length == 0) return _extraOptions;\n\n        // No caller options, return enforced\n        if (_extraOptions.length == 0) return enforced;\n\n        // @dev If caller provided _extraOptions, must be type 3 as its the ONLY type that can be combined.\n        if (_extraOptions.length >= 2) {\n            _assertOptionsType3(_extraOptions);\n            // @dev Remove the first 2 bytes containing the type from the _extraOptions and combine with enforced.\n            return bytes.concat(enforced, _extraOptions[2:]);\n        }\n\n        // No valid set of options was found.\n        revert InvalidOptions(_extraOptions);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Internal function to assert that options are of type 3.\n     * @param _options The options to be checked.\n     */\n    function _assertOptionsType3(bytes calldata _options) internal pure virtual {\n        uint16 optionsType = uint16(bytes2(_options[0:2]));\n        if (optionsType != OPTION_TYPE_3) revert InvalidOptions(_options);\n    }\n}\n"

        CREATE3 OPERATIONS                    */\n    /*.•°:°.´+˚.*°.˚:*.´•*.+°.•°:´*.´•*.•°.•°:°.´:•˚°.*°.˚:*.´+°.•*/\n\n    /// @dev Deploys `initCode` deterministically with a `salt`.\n    /// Returns the deterministic address of the deployed contract,\n    /// which solely depends on `salt`.\n    function deployDeterministic(bytes memory initCode, bytes32 salt)\n        internal\n        returns (address deployed)\n    {\n        deployed = deployDeterministic(0, initCode, salt);\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Deploys `initCode` deterministically with a `salt`.\n    /// The deployed contract is funded with `value` (in wei) ETH.\n    /// Returns the deterministic address of the deployed contract,\n    /// which solely depends on `salt`.\n    function deployDeterministic(uint256 value, bytes memory initCode, bytes32 salt)\n        internal\n        returns (address deployed)\n    {\n        /// @solidity memory-safe-assembly\n        assembly {\n            mstore(0x00, _PROXY_INITCODE) // Store the `_PROXY_INITCODE`.\n            let proxy := create2(0, 0x10, 0x10, salt)\n            if iszero(proxy) {\n                mstore(0x00, 0x30116425) // `DeploymentFailed()`.\n                revert(0x1c, 0x04)\n            }\n            mstore(0x14, proxy) // Store the proxy's address.\n            // 0xd6 = 0xc0 (short RLP prefix) + 0x16 (length of: 0x94 ++ proxy ++ 0x01).\n            // 0x94 = 0x80 + 0x14 (0x14 = the length of an address, 20 bytes, in hex).\n            mstore(0x00, 0xd694)\n            mstore8(0x34, 0x01) // Nonce of the proxy contract (1).\n            deployed := keccak256(0x1e, 0x17)\n            if iszero(\n                mul( // The arguments of `mul` are evaluated last to first.\n                    extcodesize(deployed),\n                    call(gas(), proxy, value, add(initCode, 0x20), mload(initCode), 0x00, 0x00)\n                )\n            ) {\n                mstore(0x00, 0x30116425) // `DeploymentFailed()`.\n                revert(0x1c, 0x04)\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Returns the deterministic address for `salt`.\n    function predictDeterministicAddress(bytes32 salt) internal view returns (address deployed) {\n        deployed = predictDeterministicAddress(salt, address(this));\n    }\n\n    /// @dev Returns the deterministic address for `salt` with `deployer`.\n    function predictDeterministicAddress(bytes32 salt, address deployer)\n        internal\n        pure\n        returns (address deployed)\n    {\n        /// @solidity memory-safe-assembly\n        assembly {\n            let m := mload(0x40) // Cache the free memory pointer.\n            mstore(0x00, deployer) // Store `deployer`.\n            mstore8(0x0b, 0xff) // Store the prefix.\n            mstore(0x20, salt) // Store the salt.\n            mstore(0x40, PROXY_INITCODE_HASH) // Store the bytecode hash.\n\n            mstore(0x14, keccak256(0x0b, 0x55)) // Store the proxy's address.\n            mstore(0x40, m) // Restore the free memory pointer.\n            // 0xd6 = 0xc0 (short RLP prefix) + 0x16 (length of: 0x94 ++ proxy ++ 0x01).\n            // 0x94 = 0x80 + 0x14 (0x14 = the length of an address, 20 bytes, in hex).\n            mstore(0x00, 0xd694)\n            mstore8(0x34, 0x01) // Nonce of the proxy contract (1).\n            deployed := keccak256(0x1e, 0x17)\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

Skips solidity \"index-out-of-range\" check.\n     *\n     * WARNING: Only use if you are certain `pos` is lower than the array length.\n     */\n    function unsafeAccess(address[] storage arr, uint256 pos) internal pure returns (StorageSlot.AddressSlot storage) {\n        bytes32 slot;\n        assembly (\"memory-safe\") {\n            slot := arr.slot\n        }\n        return slot.deriveArray().offset(pos).getAddressSlot();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Access an array in an \"unsafe\" way. Skips solidity \"index-out-of-range\" check.\n     *\n     * WARNING: Only use if you are certain `pos` is lower than the array length.\n     */\n    function unsafeAccess(uint256[] storage arr, uint256 pos) internal pure returns (StorageSlot.Uint256Slot storage) {\n        bytes32 slot;\n        assembly (\"memory-safe\") {\n            slot := arr.slot\n        }\n        return slot.deriveArray().offset(pos).getUint256Slot();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Access an array in an \"unsafe\" way. Skips solidity \"index-out-of-range\" check.\n     *\n     * WARNING: Only use if you are certain `pos` is lower than the array length.\n     */\n    function unsafeAccess(bytes[] storage arr, uint256 pos) internal pure returns (StorageSlot.BytesSlot storage) {\n        bytes32 slot;\n        assembly (\"memory-safe\") {\n            slot := arr.slot\n        }\n        return slot.deriveArray().offset(pos).getBytesSlot();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Access an array in an \"unsafe\" way. Skips solidity \"index-out-of-range\" check.\n     *\n     * WARNING: Only use if you are certain `pos` is lower than the array length.\n     */\n    function unsafeAccess(string[] storage arr, uint256 pos) internal pure returns (StorageSlot.StringSlot storage) {\n        bytes32 slot;\n        assembly (\"memory-safe\") {\n            slot := arr.slot\n        }\n        return slot.deriveArray().offset(pos).getStringSlot();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Access an array in an \"unsafe\" way. Skips solidity \"index-out-of-range\" check.\n     *\n     * WARNING: Only use if you are certain `pos` is lower than the array length.\n     */\n    function unsafeMemoryAccess(address[] memory arr, uint256 pos) internal pure returns (address res) {\n        assembly {\n            res := mload(add(add(arr, 0x20), mul(pos, 0x20)))\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Access an array in an \"unsafe\" way. Skips solidity \"index-out-of-range\" check.\n     *\n     * WARNING: Only use if you are certain `pos` is lower than the array length.\n     */\n    function unsafeMemoryAccess(bytes32[] memory arr, uint256 pos) internal pure returns (bytes32 res) {\n        assembly {\n            res := mload(add(add(arr, 0x20), mul(pos, 0x20)))\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Access an array in an \"unsafe\" way. Skips solidity \"index-out-of-range\" check.\n     *\n     * WARNING: Only use if you are certain `pos` is lower than the array length.\n     */\n    function unsafeMemoryAccess(uint256[] memory arr, uint256 pos) internal pure returns (uint256 res) {\n        assembly {\n            res := mload(add(add(arr, 0x20), mul(pos, 0x20)))\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Access an array in an \"unsafe\" way. Skips solidity \"index-out-of-range\" check.\n     *\n     * WARNING: Only use if you are certain `pos` is lower than the array length.\n     */\n    function unsafeMemoryAccess(bytes[] memory arr, uint256 pos) internal pure returns (bytes memory res) {\n        assembly {\n            res := mload(add(add(arr, 0x20), mul(pos, 0x20)))\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Access an array in an \"unsafe\" way. Skips solidity \"index-out-of-range\" check.\n     *\n     * WARNING: Only use if you are certain `pos` is lower than the array length.\n     */\n    function unsafeMemoryAccess(string[] memory arr, uint256 pos) internal pure returns (string memory res) {\n        assembly {\n            res := mload(add(add(arr, 0x20), mul(pos, 0x20)))\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Helper to set the length of a dynamic array. Directly writing to `.length` is forbidden.\n     *\n     * WARNING: this does not clear elements if length is reduced, of initialize elements if length is increased.\n     */\n    function unsafeSetLength(address[] storage array, uint256 len) internal {\n        assembly (\"memory-safe\") {\n            sstore(array.slot, len)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Helper to set the length of a dynamic array. Directly writing to `.length` is forbidden.\n     *\n     * WARNING: this does not clear elements if length is reduced, of initialize elements if length is increased.\n     */\n    function unsafeSetLength(bytes32[] storage array, uint256 len) internal {\n        assembly (\"memory-safe\") {\n            sstore(array.slot, len)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Helper to set the length of a dynamic array. Directly writing to `.length` is forbidden.\n     *\n     * WARNING: this does not clear elements if length is reduced, of initialize elements if length is increased.\n     */\n    function unsafeSetLength(uint256[] storage array, uint256 len) internal {\n        assembly (\"memory-safe\") {\n            sstore(array.slot, len)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Helper to set the length of a dynamic array. Directly writing to `.length` is forbidden.\n     *\n     * WARNING: this does not clear elements if length is reduced, of initialize elements if length is increased.\n     */\n    function unsafeSetLength(bytes[] storage array, uint256 len) internal {\n        assembly (\"memory-safe\") {\n            sstore(array.slot, len)\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Helper to set the length of a dynamic array. Directly writing to `.length` is forbidden.\n     *\n     * WARNING: this does not clear elements if length is reduced, of initialize elements if length is increased.\n     */\n    function unsafeSetLength(string[] storage array, uint256 len) internal {\n        assembly (\"memory-safe\") {\n            sstore(array.slot, len)\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
